Model before rewriting:

find x: tuple (bool, int(1..4))

such that

toInt(x[1]) = x[2] % 2

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#packed_1: int(0..7)

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x

--

toInt(x[1]) = x[2] % 2
   ~~> select_representation_unconstrained ([("ReprGeneral", 9900)])
toInt(x[1]) = x[2] % 2
new variables:
  find x#packed_1#rank_1: bool
  find x#packed_1#rank_2: bool
  find x#packed_1#rank_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#packed_1#rank_3),or([!(x#packed_1#rank_2),or([!(x#packed_1#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

x[1]
   ~~> tuple_index_to_bubble ([("Bubble", 8000)])
{SafeIndex(x,[1]) @ and([1 >= 1,1 <= 2;int(1..)])}

--

SafeIndex(x,[1])
   ~~> tuple_packed_index_lit ([("ReprTuplePacked", 9700)])
SafeIndex([false,true;int(1..)],[sum([(x#packed_1 / 4),1;int(1..)])])

--

SafeIndex([false,true;int(1..)],[sum([(x#packed_1 / 4),1;int(1..)])])
   ~~> introduce_element ([("SAT", 18400)])
__0
new variables:
  findAux __0: bool
new constraints:
  satElement([false,true;int(1..)], sum([(x#packed_1 / 4),1;int(1..)]), __0)

--

x#packed_1
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x#packed_1

--

x#packed_1
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_unsigned ([("SAT", 9500)])
SATInt(Rank([(0, 7)]), [x#packed_1#rank_1,x#packed_1#rank_2,x#packed_1#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 7])

--

x[2]
   ~~> tuple_index_to_bubble ([("Bubble", 8000)])
{SafeIndex(x,[2]) @ and([2 >= 1,2 <= 2;int(1..)])}

--

SafeIndex(x,[2])
   ~~> tuple_packed_index_lit ([("ReprTuplePacked", 9700)])
sum([x#packed_1 % 4,1;int(1..)])

--

x#packed_1
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x#packed_1

--

x#packed_1
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_unsigned ([("SAT", 9500)])

--

(SATInt(Rank([(0, 7)]), [x#packed_1#rank_1,x#packed_1#rank_2,x#packed_1#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 7]) / 4)
   ~~> div_to_bubble ([("Bubble", 6000)])
{SafeDiv(SATInt(Rank([(0, 7)]), [x#packed_1#rank_1,x#packed_1#rank_2,x#packed_1#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 7]), 4) @ 4 != 0}

--

   ~~> encode_sat_int_literals ([("SAT", 9400)])

--

   ~~> mod_to_bubble ([("Bubble", 6000)])
{SafeMod(SATInt(Rank([(0, 7)]), [x#packed_1#rank_1,x#packed_1#rank_2,x#packed_1#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 7]),4) @ 4 != 0}

--

SafeMod(SATInt(Rank([(0, 7)]), [x#packed_1#rank_1,x#packed_1#rank_2,x#packed_1#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 7]),4)
   ~~> encode_sat_int_literals ([("SAT", 9400)])

--

sum([SafeMod(SATInt(Rank([(0, 7)]), [x#packed_1#rank_1,x#packed_1#rank_2,x#packed_1#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 7]),SATInt(Rank([(4, 4)]), [false;int(1..)] [4, 4])),1;int(1..)]) % 2
   ~~> mod_to_bubble ([("Bubble", 6000)])
{SafeMod(sum([SafeMod(SATInt(Rank([(0, 7)]), [x#packed_1#rank_1,x#packed_1#rank_2,x#packed_1#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 7]),SATInt(Rank([(4, 4)]), [false;int(1..)] [4, 4])),1;int(1..)]),2) @ 2 != 0}
